# Decisions log

One line per decision in [decisions.md](decisions.md) added or changed, newest at the bottom. What was built is in git history.

Add a line at the end of the file. Git merges this file by union (`.gitattributes`), so lines two agents add at once both land with no conflict; that is why the log has a file of its own rather than sitting under the table.

- **2026-10-12**: D188 and D189 added (Decided): texture budgets for characters and gear; packs as a bag and a harness, with belt items hung from standard points and swinging loose.
- **2026-10-12**: D31 changed: the developers asked to move to Bevy 0.20 now, before `bevy-inspector-egui` has a release for it; the inspector is pinned to the commit of its open port (jakobhellermann/bevy-inspector-egui#324) until one comes. D12 now names 0.20.
- **2026-10-12**: The art direction moved from direction D's flat colour to textured, lightly stylised realism (D14), after the developers compared a log cabin both ways: models keep their detailed textures, a little shaded, neither photoreal nor cartoon. art-style.md rewritten; its concept prompt now asks for that. A full cabin scene with every piece textured is next.
- **2026-10-12**: The scene made more real (D190): the ground drawn from CC0 soil, grass, rock and sand textures, triplanar and tinted by the planet; the shade lit by the sky and the ground's bounce rather than a flat ambient; more natural ground colours; ACES tone mapping and a less washed-out sky. Materials' sources are CC0 libraries first, Tripo for what they lack (D191), fetched and stacked by `tools/textures`.
- **2026-10-12**: A stone kit (D179, built): stone walls laid as models, metre sections, half blocks and cornerstones, with a timber lintel over openings, mixing with logs per cube, logs ending flush against stone. A base is laid by every kit at once. `tools/props` gained `reference`, `grade` and `block`, so a kit's pieces share one concept and grade and a block reads alike from every side. Doors and windows are no longer shown in a plan, as the developers said a plan has none; logs are cut back where they would pass through a roof.
- **2026-10-12**: Rules for construction pieces proposed (D192, Open), Enshrouded's way on our cubes: [construction-001.md](proposals/construction-001.md), with every case walked through, after [research/adjacency-pieces.md](../research/adjacency-pieces.md). Built meanwhile: log corners within the grid, and collision by state, ghosts walked through.
- **2026-10-12**: Gaps in the construction plan found and answered in [construction-001.md](proposals/construction-001.md#gaps-and-how-to-close-them) (D192, still Open): thirty, from a review of the docs, decisions and code; the technical answers mine, the design calls added as questions. Doors and windows are props fitted into holes that fit them, as the developers said. D178 brought in line: log ends at the corner cube's face.
- **2026-10-12**: The developers answered construction-001's questions 8 to 14 (D193): nothing is taken away while something is attached to it or rests on it; lost support collapses into debris; cubes may be stairs, slopes and halves; ground is cut only inside a building's footprint (D131 narrowed); plans have a budget per claim and are seen by the tribe; refunds are exact and go to the claim's storage when the taker did not pay; claims do not overlap and everything built stays inside them.
- **2026-10-12**: Construction 001 adopted (D192), but for the dual grid for block kinds, which waits on its spike. The developers left questions 1 to 7 to me (D194): footings to the ground, costed by the cube and laid by the game; a cube's fill the unit of building; corners as each material is; any thickness; which kinds are blocks; nothing floats in the MVP (D142 refined). D160 changed: progress per cube with a fill level, layouts that follow the current kit, merged meshes per section, snapshots, ground sampled into the plan.
- **2026-10-12**: The construction system's technology plan written (D195): [building-plan.md](building-plan.md), at the developers' request, from construction-001 (D192 to D194) and AGENTS.md's rules.
- **2026-10-12**: D196 added: when Bevy falls short, port the version of an engine that does it well, from its source where the licence allows; now a core tenet in `AGENTS.md`. D184 points to it. D192 to D195 moved from the superseded table into the live one, where they belong.
- **2026-10-12**: D23 now pins Blender too, at 5.2 in `tools/blender/version`, and `cargo xtask check` checks the Blender scripts (D37), from [research/blender-pipeline-practice.md](../research/blender-pipeline-practice.md): `run.sh` took whatever Blender was newest, and 5,100 lines of Python were outside the gate.
- **2026-10-12**: Block modules spiked ([research/block-modules.md](../research/block-modules.md)): conformed Tripo pieces read as a grid; a bevelled, displaced stone surface on the cubes reads as one natural wall. Recommended as how block kinds are drawn (D197, Open, an exception to D177). The kit sheet now stands any `--model` beside it, and logs vantages on the ground.
- **2026-10-12**: The By column added to every decision, at the developers' request: "if decision is made by AI and not human, that's ok but it needs to be noted so and treated more like a soft decision". Filled by reading each row's Why: 120 by the developers, 56 by an agent (10 of them Design: D13, D43, D105, D114, D130, D158, D171, D175, D178, D194). The developers can correct any of them.
- **2026-10-12**: D197 Decided by the developers and built: block kinds drawn as their cubes' own surface, rounded and displaced by their material (`terra_build::surface`); the stone kit's model sections removed. The kit sheet has stone and logs meeting every way walls meet: an L, a T each way, in line, pillars in a log wall, a stone end wall.
- **2026-10-12**: D37: `cargo xtask check` now runs as a git pre-commit hook, at the developers' request ("set it up"); before, the rule was only written down.
- **2026-10-13**: D198 added (Decided, by the agent): the game split into world, walker and gameplay plugin layers, at the developers' request, with the sandbox's `walk` running the first two alone.
- **2026-10-13**: Step 1 of the building plan built (D195, D199): `terra_build` split into `cubes` (sections of 16, cubes packed in 32 bits, sorted, dirty marks), `ops` (operations, each checked and with its inverse, refusals in words), `ground`, `support`, `rooms`, `cost` (exact fractions) and `save` (snapshots, and older saves read, moved from the game); property tests over random operations. The game unchanged on top.
- **2026-10-13**: D198 and D28: the world layer moved into its own crate, `terra_world` (the shell, sound, the planet, terrain, the start and the world plugins), which may use Bevy; it builds, tests and runs alone (`cargo run -p terra_world --example world`).
- **2026-10-12**: D200 added (Open, the agent's recommendation): the timber walls spike compared generated round logs, generated hewn timber and timber as surface against the Tripo logs; [research/timber-walls.md](../research/timber-walls.md) recommends generated long members. The spike is kept until the developers judge it.
- **2026-10-13**: D201 added (Decided, by the agent): step 4 of the building plan built: plans laid, built and taken apart through checked operations with their refusals shown, doors and windows fitted as props, saves as snapshots, `build.*` remote methods and `cargo xtask cabin`. D199's shortcuts now build only villages, the kit sheet and the build keys' example.
- **2026-10-13**: D199: what a base keeps costs in is bounded (powers-of-two shares, no finer than 1/65536, no more than 2^24 items) and a snapshot past those bounds is refused, since a hostile one could overflow the exact fractions.
- **2026-10-13**: D185: benchmarks now hold each scene to the frame budget (share over, hitches, drift of the game's work) and fail when one misses, at the developers' request ("check whether we can sustain good performance on the machine while the character does a simple walk in the forest"); a `walk` scene on a scripted route was added, and scripted runs on macOS keep out of App Nap, which had throttled any run past 40 s.
- **2026-10-13**: D200 Decided by the developers: timber drawn as generated long members, round logs in their bark the log kit and hewn timber a second profile, each log varying by seed; built in `terra_build::timber` from the kit file, the spike and the Tripo log models deleted. D177 restated with its two exceptions (D197, D200). `terra_build`'s layouts split into `kit`, `layout` (`runs`, `trims`, `ground`, `floors`, `blocks`), `timber`, `draw` and `relief` (D195, step 2). Kit materials reflect 2% rather than 4%, so the sky no longer sheens them blue in shade.
- **2026-10-13**: D202 added (Decided, by the agent): ground cover is `terra_world::cover`, grass blades after Ghost of Tsushima round the camera and the ground under them painted by the same field (litter, moss); the lush spike's grass and path ribbons replaced by it. Scripted runs can take a sequence of screenshots (`--shots`) and move a fixed camera between views (`--vantage …@frame`), and `cargo xtask vantage` makes the vantage sheet (D69).
